# Per-case tables, x86-64 CI

Ratios are baseline time over candidate time (median of the paired windows) on each of the three hosts of a run of the `x86-64 paired benchmark` workflow; higher is faster. Rows are sorted by the median over the hosts. `Baseline ns/doc` is the median of the hosts' baseline times. Generated by `harness/tables.py` from `results/x86/`.

Runs:

- [`aa`: A/A control, #419's head `a4d2fd0a` against itself](#aa-aa-control-419s-head-a4d2fd0a-against-itself)
- [`414`: #414 ASCII-only block trimming `7eda5a4b`, base `aceafd32`](#414-414-ascii-only-block-trimming-7eda5a4b-base-aceafd32)
- [`426`: #426 first commit `09538a76`, dispatch inlined, base `af65cb43`](#426-426-first-commit-09538a76-dispatch-inlined-base-af65cb43)
- [`426b`: #426 revision `b64cc1e6`, dispatch out of line, base `af65cb43`](#426b-426-revision-b64cc1e6-dispatch-out-of-line-base-af65cb43)
- [`428`: #428 first commit `bdde4db0`, AVX2 at every size, base `05a55a01`](#428-428-first-commit-bdde4db0-avx2-at-every-size-base-05a55a01)
- [`428b`: #428 revision `2bf6ba56`, run 1, base `05a55a01`](#428b-428-revision-2bf6ba56-run-1-base-05a55a01)
- [`428c`: #428 revision `2bf6ba56`, run 2, base `05a55a01`](#428c-428-revision-2bf6ba56-run-2-base-05a55a01)
- [`430`: #430 `5782b91c` inlined SSE2 escape classifiers, base `f8fff4c9`](#430-430-5782b91c-inlined-sse2-escape-classifiers-base-f8fff4c9)
- [`431`: #431 first commit `273b6bf6`, whole scan out of line, base `dfcfe983`](#431-431-first-commit-273b6bf6-whole-scan-out-of-line-base-dfcfe983)
- [`431b`: #431 revision `d6e0bad3`, inline 16-byte probe, base `dfcfe983`](#431b-431-revision-d6e0bad3-inline-16-byte-probe-base-dfcfe983)
- [`431inl`: #431 comparison `78429a94`, fully inline, base `dfcfe983`](#431inl-431-comparison-78429a94-fully-inline-base-dfcfe983)
- [`431pl`: #431 placebo `41f4e99e`, base `dfcfe983`](#431pl-431-placebo-41f4e99e-base-dfcfe983)
